- **click-argument-suppression**: Click injects one function parameter per CLI option. Commands with 5+ options inherently exceed CodeScene's "Excess Number of Function Arguments" threshold of 4. ([humansintheloop-dev/humansintheloop-dev-workflow-and-tools](https://github.com/humansintheloop-dev/humansintheloop-dev-workflow-and-tools/blob/HEAD/design-pattern-catalog/commands/click-argument-suppression.md) · ★49)
